GAP / functional–molecular analysis

Molecular coordinates of in-vivo neural function

Which reproducible components of a neuron's response law are readable from molecular identity, continuous molecular state, anatomy, projection or structural context—and what should be measured next?

Article logic

One causal progression from molecular coordinate to experiment design

The seven figures are not independent benchmarks. Each Figure creates the evidence contract required by the next one; a negative or blocked result remains visible and prevents downstream overclaiming.

Fig1 · Readable coordinateIdentity, continuous genes, space and structure under held units. Fig2 · Recover stateQualify reference → lift endpoints → test held-unit geometry. Fig3 · Measure efficientlyFixed budget, matched random, true-hidden validation. Fig4 · Complete functionManifold headroom + unique molecular information. Fig5 · Learn response lawDirect prediction versus stronger shared/operator structure. Fig6 · Test reuseLabel efficiency, same-species and cross-species transfer. Fig7 · Choose interventionFreeze model, nominate perturbation, prospectively falsify.
Single inference rule: animal / fish / worm / donor when available—not cell count. Single reporting rule: main support, independent replication and boundary are all shown. Tool execution PASS is not automatically a positive biological result.
FIGURE 1 / IDENTITY → STATE → CONTEXT

Figure 1 — What molecular coordinate is readable?

Disentangle discrete identity, continuous molecular state, position and structural context under the same held-biological-unit split.

1. IdentityAsk how much coarse molecular class explains.
2. Continuous stateTest genes within/beyond identity.
3. ContextAdd position/connectivity only when supported.
4. Multi-axis inferenceKeep R², rank, classification, AIC and effect size separate.
Supported claim

Functional readability is layered and dataset-specific; continuous molecular state can add information below identity, while other systems are dominated by space or structural coordinates.

Boundary / not claimed

No universal 'genes always win' claim; session-held MICRONS is not cross-animal molecular evidence; different metrics are not collapsed into one score.

Figure and result gallery

Requirement-aligned panel map; current numerical authority is tabulated below.
Held-unit coordinate balance across Bugeon, Xu and Shainer.
Zhao metric dissociation: tiny variance gain can coexist with strong rank/classification structure.

FIGURE 2 / REFERENCE BRIDGE → LIFT → GEOMETRY

Figure 2 — When can missing molecular state be recovered?

Qualify the reference first, then test endpoint lift, then test whether held-unit functional geometry is actually recovered.

1. Reference QCExact anatomy and gene overlap before any lift.
2. Endpoint liftAsk whether specific functional endpoints improve.
3. GeometryTest held-unit global functional relationships.
4. NullsUse matched random mapping / gene selection.
Supported claim

AtlasLift is supported only in matched regimes: WARP and exact PERI/ECT provide strong held-unit examples; exact-region references reduce false optimism.

Boundary / not claimed

Reference self-recovery alone is not functional proof. Bugeon/Shainer/Condylis geometry boundaries remain visible even when mapping or endpoint recovery is useful.

FIGURE 3 / FIXED BUDGET → MATCHED RANDOM → HIDDEN VALIDATION

Figure 3 — What should a compact assay measure?

Separate measured-panel compression, true hidden-gene replacement, prospective marker nomination and broad replication.

1. BudgetFix K before comparing panels.
2. Matched randomCompare selected K against matched-K random.
3. Hidden validationUse truly measured held-out genes when available.
4. ReplicationDo not call a selector universal from one dataset.
Supported claim

Compact molecular measurement can preserve useful functional structure, but the winning panel depends on objective and evidence tier.

Boundary / not claimed

Compression is not replacement; prospective atlas nomination is not measured hidden-gene validation; M1 provenance remains explicitly pending.

Figure and result gallery

Projection operator is amplitude-poor (Direct R2=-0.027; best shared R2=-0.013) but shared structure improves rank/AUC (delta Spearman=+0.122); projection relation q=0.329.Do not convert relative rank/AUC improvement with negative absolute R2 into a projection-prediction success; HCR<->Zhao visual semantic transfer also remains negative.
FIGURE 4 / MANIFOLD → HEADROOM → BEHLIFT → NEXT MEASUREMENT

Figure 4 — Molecularly readable functional manifolds and conditional completion

Ask whether molecular coordinates organize a functional manifold and whether they add unique information about an omitted behavior after other functions are known.

1. ManifoldLiftPredict held-unit latent / topology, not decorative embeddings.
2. HeadroomMeasure what other functions already explain.
3. BehLiftAdd molecular state only on identical held-unit folds.
4. Next measurementConvert retrospective headroom into experiment-design priority.
Supported claim

C. elegans, Xu, Bugeon, Sorensen, WARP and Allen VC2P establish multiple forms of molecularly organized function; the strongest result depends on headroom and representation.

Boundary / not claimed

A weak global manifold does not erase strong selected-axis completion. Condylis saturation, Zhao native negative completion, sparse-marker Shainer/Habenula and non-molecular NeuroPlex remain boundaries.

FIGURE 5 / DIRECT MAPPING → SHARED LAW → PROGRAMS → STRUCTURAL BRANCH

Figure 5 — Molecular state parameterizes response laws and structural coordinates

Separate direct predictability from the stronger claim that a shared/low-rank response law improves over independent heads.

1. Direct mappingCan molecules read response or structural coordinates?
2. Shared operatorSame X, same Y, same folds versus independent Ridge.
3. ProgramsInterpret receptor/transmitter/state susceptibility.
4. Structural branchProjection/connectivity predictability is a separate law.
Supported claim

Bugeon anchors the functional shared-law result; Xu and Sorensen replicate functionally; Chevee, worm, MERGE-seq and Sorensen establish structural predictability; Sorensen provides the strongest shared structural-law replication.

Boundary / not claimed

Strong direct predictability does not guarantee shared-operator gain. Negative absolute R² cannot be rescued into a success by a positive delta alone.

FIGURE 6 / SAMPLE EFFICIENCY → SAME-SPECIES TRANSFER → CROSS-SPECIES → HUMAN

Figure 6 — Selective reuse, label efficiency and cross-species conservation

Treat strict zero-shot, few-shot, matched representation, mechanism ablation and evolutionary geometry as distinct contracts.

1. EfficiencyDoes frozen structure reduce paired-label demand?
2. Same speciesZhao→Bugeon with source-only selection.
3. Cross speciesMouse/fish/worm directions and target-held evaluation.
4. HumanDonor-aware intrinsic-ephys endpoint; keep source uncertainty.
Supported claim

Selected molecular-functional programs transfer when semantic axis and molecular geometry are aligned; same-species and mouse→fish/human results provide the strongest frozen evidence.

Boundary / not claimed

No universal foundation replacement claim. Failed HCR→Zhao transfer, weak native-dataset foundation-forward tests and anatomy/provenance exclusions remain part of the evidence.

FIGURE 7 / PERTURBATION RESPONSE → FROZEN GAP → NOMINATION → CAUSAL TEST

Figure 7 — Perturb-GAP prospective experiment design

Project distributed molecular perturbation responses through a frozen response-law model to choose experiments that are selective, falsifying or context-discriminating.

1. FreezeNo refitting after nomination.
2. Distributed ΔGUse observed/network response, not target-only fantasy.
3. Rank experimentsSelectivity, model disagreement, context disagreement.
4. Causal closureNew biological units must move preregistered coordinates.
Supported claim

Whole-brain Perturb-seq shows that context×perturbation interaction dominates projected functional effects (85.48%), motivating context-specific prospective experiments.

Boundary / not claimed

This is nomination, not causal proof. Missing-edge prediction remains weak and no real perturbation has yet closed the causal loop.

Figure and result gallery

Prospective causal-closure panel map.
Context×perturbation interaction dominates projected functional effects.
End-to-end gate matrix linking upstream evidence to prospective use.

Three prospective experiment classes

Selective coordinate nominations

State-running: Syn2 / Nlgn2 / Sec23a. Speed: Myo6 / Apc / Cntnap2. Pupil: Ift43 / Slc7a14 / Klf13. Self-memory: Rest / Atp6v1a / Ppargc1b.

Model-falsification nominations

Htr3a in L4-5 IT CTX Glut: cosine -0.911. Cck in Pvalb Gaba: cosine -0.784; target-only and network-aware predictions disagree.

Context-dependence nominations

Notch4 Pvalb vs MB Glut: cosine -0.974; Gprc5b and Pax7 examples similarly discordant.

Open full Figure 7 authority

Missing-edge boundary: global held-edge functional R² is ~0; two-way additive and low-rank residual models do not close the interaction tensor. Large interaction is not equivalent to easy prediction.
RESULTS ATLAS · COMPLETE DATASET ROLE MATRIX

Results Atlas — all article-supporting datasets

Every dataset gets an explicit manuscript role: main support, independent replication, ED breadth, boundary, provenance guardrail or tool QA. Derived aliases do not count as independent replication.

Main chainOnly results that advance the biological logic.
Independent replicationDistinct dataset/species support.
BoundaryNegative/saturation/N/A states remain visible.
Tool QAExecution breadth is not counted as biological replication.
Supported claim

Dataset roles are selected by relevance → multimetric agreement → held-unit replication → independent-dataset value → effect size.

Boundary / not claimed

Execution PASS does not mean biological-positive; derived aliases and scoped variants never inflate independent replication count.

Tool contract

What happens when a genuinely new dataset arrives?

The public interface is capability-aware. It first decides what the file means, then what the science permits. “Blocked” is a valid result when the required evidence does not exist.

0 · Semantic intakegene / function / projection / type / space / QC?
1 · Integritystable cell ID, duplicate scope, biological unit, fold support
2 · Capability routerun only scientifically applicable stages
3 · Figure 1genes/type/space/structure on identical held-unit splits
4 · Figure 2rank matched references; qualify bridge before lift
5 · Figure 3fixed budget + matched-K random + hidden-gene boundary

Execution coverage · current authority

External stress test • EASI-PASS 2026

Incomplete public data should produce a correct stop—not a fabricated biology result

Direct method-level reproduction

1199 / 1439 = 83.3%

accepted IoU matches; 1149/1439=79.85% confident soma-print; median z-restricted IoU 0.286.

authors' released JS078 golden output • preprint DOI

Three-level intake

matching/QC only → all Fig1–3 biology stages safe-stop.

aligned molecular table → auto canonicalize PV→Pvalb, SST→Sst; exact VISp AtlasLift can run.

multi-animal molecular+function → applicable Fig1–3 stages open under held animals.

Claim boundary

The released demo does not contain the full PBN/V1 functional biology dataset. PBN response specificity and V1 arousal/correlation claims therefore remain READY WITH FULL DATA, not “reproduced.”

PBN engineering branch

96 cells = 24 repeated local IDs × 4 animals remain 96 scoped identities; 4 held animals; exact PBN reference auto-selected; function-map/coordinate/fixed-budget/AIC/effect-size execute; one-dimensional response correctly blocks Zhao-style relation-index.

V1 paper-native pairwise branch

120 cells / 4 animals / raw aligned EASI metadata + traces. Pairwise inference is one slope/rank statistic per animal: within-class median ρ=-0.812, across-class -0.828. Engineering fixture only.

External-readiness authority · paper-native replication contract

Composability

Figure 1–3 are now the upstream contract for Figure 4–7

Tool success does not mean every later biological result is positive. Allen Visual Learning HCR reaches Figure 7 through one public workflow while preserving mixed/negative gates.
